for a valid development profile. The [D1 gap map](GENERAL_CONTACT_D1_ACCEPTANCE_GAP_MAP.md)
and this ledger retain local profile-family test evidence; applications must
not turn `validation.valid` or `implemented` into user-facing admission.

not a complete tangent or pressure-space acceptance claim.

also passed independent review with separate force and moment scales at the

An eight-Q4 distributed-force press against the partially supported,
deformable two-S3 target is [locally tested in development](reference_cases/general_contact_d1_free_target_press_development.md),
not independently accepted. Three runs with the ordinary 1e-6 inner
force tolerance failed at first contact despite passing gap and pressure
rank checks. Holding mechanics, pressure criteria and augmentation limit
fixed while tightening only inner equilibrium to 1e-8 completed all three
press steps. Each contact step accepted one supported-Schur correction;
the same eight-Q4 setup then locally completed a 12-step
press/slide/open/recontact cycle. A fresh-model six/twelve split restart
reproduced all saved integrated-force, gap and multiplier-change scalars;
an uninterrupted development replay subsequently matched all twelve
ordered pressure-feature IDs and multiplier vectors exactly against that
split run. Independent integrated-response reference and coupled refinement
remain open.
A later actual free-target equilibrium pose passed a selected mixed
source/target smooth directional development check at 4e-7 and 2e-7 m;
an 8e-7 m comparison crossed pressure activation and remains inconclusive.
The prospective 3e-7/1.5e-7 m [formal gate and independent review](reference_cases/general_contact_d1_free_target_equilibrium_tangent_v1_review.md)
accepted **only this selected fixed-multiplier smooth tangent direction**
after a separately checked one-field source-hash amendment. Complete
tangent acceptance remains open.
The first sixteen-Q4 free-target source-refinement press failed its unchanged
pressure consistency gate at step three after two nonlinear Schur proposals
were rejected on re-equilibration. Its force, gap and pressure-rank checks
passed, but it supplies no accepted sixteen-Q4 response; the local
pressure-response diagnostic is the next action. The raw failure and bounded
interpretation are retained in the free-target development record.
The subsequent disposable 2%-fraction diagnostic matched the constrained
gap-response prediction within 3.075e−14 m on the unchanged two-mode branch.
This supports the local derivative while leaving finite-step/branch
acceleration and the failed sixteen-Q4 path unresolved.
Disposable full, 0.75 and 0.80 fraction captures then found a 0.80
re-equilibrated proposal that passed the unchanged 25% merit rule and let
the diagnostic three-step press finish. This is a candidate safeguard only;
the production route and sixteen-Q4 response qualification remain open.
The new opt-in owner backtrack completed a three-step sixteen-Q4 free-target
press under unchanged mechanics and contact gates, including the reviewed
overlap/clip-branch guard. Its accepted full-load contact force differs by
8.31% from eight Q4, so mesh response and C1 acceptance are still open.
Its twelve-step press/slide/open/recontact development cycle also completed.
The twenty-four-Q4 free-target press remains unaccepted: three- and six-step
paths fail the unchanged pressure-consistency gate at full load. A bounded
secant-informed retry improves an earlier pressure transition, but does not
resolve that full-load failure. The raw failed routes and next diagnostic
are retained in the free-target development record.

An explicitly selected `macro_q1_pair_dev` pressure basis is now locally
implemented for ordered even-cell Q4-source/S3-target Jet2 augmented patches.
It transforms shared mortar integrals before the pressure law while retaining
fine Q4 physical geometry and its independent gap certificate. The three-step
eight- and twenty-four-Q4 free-target development presses completed after an
internal force-equilibrium resolution tied to the existing explicit pressure
tolerance was added. The twenty-four-Q4 run had 26/26 supported modes and
passed its local gap, pressure, equilibrium, integration and wrench checks.
The 8/16/24-Q4 macro reactions do not converge monotonically, and each
full-load state has only one active pressure mode on shrinking support. This row is
**implemented and locally tested only**; response convergence, full cycle,
restart, installed package and independent acceptance remain open. The
[development record](reference_cases/general_contact_d1_free_target_press_development.md)
retains the initial failed macro trial and both later solves.
On the finite-area distributed eight-Q4 press, macro pressure cut the two
recorded scaled Schur conditions by about 5.45× and 6.51× and shifted the
integrated force by 1.09% versus fine Q1. Its reference-surface pressure
field differed from fine Q1 by 29.48% in L2 and was farther from the existing
24-Q4 fine field. This is a development discriminator, not pressure-space
acceptance; see the [comparison record](reference_cases/general_contact_d1_macro_distributed_press_development.md).
At the flat eight-Q4 distributed full-press pose, changing the fixed planar
target from two to four S3 faces left the integrated force unchanged to saved
precision and changed the source pressure field by only 5.06e−13 relative
L2. This [single target-partition development check](reference_cases/general_contact_d1_target_partition_development.md)
does not settle source pressure convergence or general target-mesh admission.
One further [32-Q4 fine-source press](reference_cases/general_contact_d1_source_pressure_32q4_development.md)
passed its local force, gap, multiplier, rank and integration gates, but the
reference-surface pressure L2 change increased from 7.04% at 16→24 Q4 to
13.82% at 24→32 Q4. The difference is concentrated near the moving contact
front; the established-contact interior improved. Integrated force changed
only 0.0208% from 24 Q4. Pressure-space stability and complete C1 admission
remain open.
An independent [fixed-pose affine-front comparison](reference_cases/general_contact_d1_fixed_front_pressure_development.md)
matched analytic nodal weighted-gap pressures within 1e−8 Pa and reduced the
local-plane pressure-field L2 error from 10.50% to 1.37% across 8/16/32 Q4
source columns. This bounds the base smooth-pose mortar behavior; it does not
resolve the coupled front difference or extend C1 admission.
A [saved coupled-pose comparison](reference_cases/general_contact_d1_coupled_front_pose_development.md)
found a 6.86 nm reference-front L2 gap difference between 16 and 32 Q4, while
the multiplier changed 0.02552 Pa in the same measure and the first active
source node shifted left. Different step counts and the absence of an exact
coupled pressure reference limit that result. Complete C1 pressure stability
remains open.
The same saved sequence's maximum cumulative-reaction difference increased
from 1.02% (16→24 Q4) to 1.50% (24→32 Q4), so an integrated spatial-pressure
measure cannot currently close that gate either.
